\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{1,3}&:=\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}\left(\dfrac{|\mathbb{M}-\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}|}{|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}|}\right)^{4s^{\prime}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2s^{\prime}}}\\
&\lesssim\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{r}}\left(\dfrac{|\mathbb{M}-\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}|}{|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}|}\right)^{4s^{\prime}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2s^{\prime}}}\lesssim\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|^{2}_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(B_{r})}.\end{split}

Also, assume
\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(B_{r})}\leq\kappa_{4}
for some small
\kappa_{4}=\kappa_{4}(n,p,\Lambda)
and then use Lemma
2.5
, together with
|\Omega_{r}|\eqsim|B_{r}|
from (
2.6
) with the help of (
2.4
) to have

(3.27)

\displaystyle I_{1,4}:=\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}\left(\dfrac{\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}}{\omega^{p}}+\frac{\omega^{p}}{\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}}+\frac{\omega^{p^{\prime}}}{\omega^{p^{\prime}}_{B_{r}}}\right)^{2s^{\prime}}\,dx\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{r}}\left(\dfrac{\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}}{\omega^{p}}+\frac{\omega^{p}}{\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}}+\frac{\omega^{p^{\prime}}}{\omega^{p^{\prime}}_{B_{r}}}\right)^{2s^{\prime}}\,dx\leq c.

Then by Hölder’s inequality and the above two displays, we obtain

(3.28)

\displaystyle I_{1,2}

\displaystyle\leq c\,I_{1,3}I_{1,4}^{\frac{1}{2s^{\prime}}}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}(|\nabla z|^{p}\omega^{p})^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}\lesssim c\,\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|^{2}_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(B_{r})}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}(|\nabla z|^{p}\omega^{p})^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}.

From now on, we only specify the necessary tools to provide each resulting estimates, since we mainly follow the proof of
[
BDGN21
, Proposition 17]
, and when we use the measure density property, we apply the similar manipulation as above. First, by (
3.11
), Lemma
2.8
and Lemma
2.5
together with the measure density property of
\Omega_{r}
to
B_{r}
, we estimate
I_{2}
as follows:

(3.29)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{2}&\leq\sigma\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}\varphi_{|\nabla z|}(|\nabla z-\nabla h|)\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}\,dx\\
&\quad+c_{\sigma}\varepsilon\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}(|\nabla z|^{p}\omega^{p})^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}+c_{\sigma}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{p-1}}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}\left(\dfrac{|u|^{p}}{R^{p}}\omega^{p}\right)^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}\end{split}

for any
\sigma,\varepsilon\in(0,1)
. To estimate
I_{3}
, using Young’s inequality and
0\leq\eta\leq 1
, we have

(3.30)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{3}&\lesssim\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{p-1}}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}\dfrac{\omega^{p^{\prime}}}{\omega^{p^{\prime}}_{B_{r}}}\eta^{p}|F|^{p}\omega^{p}\,dx+\varepsilon\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}|\nabla z-\nabla h|^{p}\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}\,dx+\varepsilon\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}\left|\dfrac{z-h}{r}\right|^{p}\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}\,dx\\
&=:I_{3,1}+I_{3,2}+I_{3,3}.\end{split}

Extending
z-h
as
0
in
B_{r}\setminus\Omega
, and using Proposition 3 in
[
BDGN21
]
, we have
I_{3,3}\lesssim I_{3,2}
. We employ triangle inequality, minimizing property of
h
in (
3.20
), (
3.15
), Hölder’s inequality and Lemma
2.5
together with
|\Omega_{r}|\eqsim|B_{r}|
, to obtain

(3.31)

\displaystyle I_{3,2}\leq\varepsilon c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}(|\nabla z|^{p}\omega^{p})^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}.

Using also Hölder’s inequality and Lemma
2.5
with
|\Omega_{r}|\eqsim|B_{r}|
, we have

(3.32)

\displaystyle I_{3,1}\lesssim c\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{p-1}}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}(|F|^{p}\omega^{p})^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}.

Finally, to estimate
I_{4}
, instead of dividing the case into
p>2
and
1<p\leq 2
as in
[
BDGN21
]
, we consider the cases in a unified way. By
p^{\prime}(p-2+\frac{1}{p})=p-1
and
\eta^{p^{\prime}}\nabla u=\nabla z+g
, we first see that

(3.33)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{4}&\leq\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}|\nabla(\eta^{p})||\mathcal{A}(x,\nabla u)||z-h|\,dx\\
&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}|\nabla\eta||\eta^{p^{\prime}}\nabla u|^{p-2+\frac{1}{p}}|\nabla u|^{1-\frac{1}{p}}|z-h|\omega^{p}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}\dfrac{r}{R}|\nabla z+g|^{p-2+\frac{1}{p}}|\nabla u|^{1-\frac{1}{p}}\left|\dfrac{z-h}{r}\right|\omega^{p}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\varepsilon\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}|\nabla z+g|^{p}\omega^{p}\,dx+C^{*}(\varepsilon)\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}\left(\dfrac{r}{R}\right)^{\frac{p^{2}}{p-1}}|\nabla u|^{p}\omega^{p}\,dx+\varepsilon\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}\left|\dfrac{z-h}{r}\right|^{p}\omega^{p}\,dx\end{split}

for any
\varepsilon\in(0,1]
, where for the last step we have used Young’s inequality for the exponents
\left(\frac{p}{p-2+\frac{1}{p}},\frac{p^{2}}{p-1},p\right)
and
0\leq\eta_{1}\leq 1
. Here,
C^{*}(t):(0,1]\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{+}
is such that

(3.34)

\displaystyle C^{*}(t)=\max\left\{t^{1-p},t^{-\frac{1}{p-1}}\right\},

which is a continuous function on
(0,1]
for each fixed
p\in(1,\infty)
. Recalling (
3.11
), we have

(3.35)

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}|\nabla z+g|^{p}\omega^{p}\,dx\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}|\nabla z|^{p}\omega^{p}\,dx+\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}\left|\dfrac{u}{R}\right|^{p}\omega^{p}\,dx.

Also, since
\frac{p^{2}}{p-1}>p
on
p\in(1,\infty)
and
r\leq R
hold, there holds

(3.36)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}\left(\dfrac{r}{R}\right)^{\frac{p^{2}}{p-1}}|\nabla u|^{p}\omega^{p}\,dx&\lesssim\left(\dfrac{r}{R}\right)^{p}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}|\nabla u|^{p}\omega^{p}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\left(\dfrac{r}{R}\right)^{p}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2\Omega_{r}}\left|\dfrac{u}{r}\right|^{p}\omega^{p}\,dx+\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2\Omega_{r}}|F|^{p}\omega^{p}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2\Omega_{r}}\left|\dfrac{u}{R}\right|^{p}\omega^{p}\,dx+\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2\Omega_{r}}|F|^{p}\omega^{p}\,dx.\end{split}

Extending
z-h
as
0
in
B_{r}\setminus\Omega
, and using Proposition 3 in
[
BDGN21
]
, Hölder’s inequality, Lemma
2.5
together with
|\Omega_{r}|\eqsim|B_{r}|
, triangle inequality, minimizing property of
h
in (
3.20
) and (
3.15
) yields

(3.37)

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}\left|\dfrac{z-h}{r}\right|^{p}\omega^{p}\,dx\lesssim\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}(|\nabla z|^{p}\omega^{p})^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}},

as in
[
BDGN21
]
. Note that the argument used in
[
BDGN21
]
for (
3.37
) can be applied in all cases
p\in(1,\infty)
.
Thus it follows that

(3.38)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{3}\lesssim\varepsilon\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}(|\nabla z|^{p}\omega^{p})^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}+C^{*}(\varepsilon)\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2\Omega_{r}}\left|\dfrac{u}{R}\right|^{p}\omega^{p}\,dx+C^{*}(\varepsilon)\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2\Omega_{r}}|F|^{p}\omega^{p}\,dx.\end{split}

Summing up the above all estimates, we have

(3.39)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla h)-\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla z)|^{2}\,dx&\lesssim\sigma\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}\varphi_{|\nabla z|}(|\nabla z-\nabla h|)\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}\,dx\\
&\quad+c(\sigma)(\,\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|^{2}_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(B_{r})}+\varepsilon)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}(|\nabla z|^{p}\omega^{p})^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}\\
&\quad+c(\sigma)\big(\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{p-1}}+C^{*}(\varepsilon)\big)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2\Omega_{r}}\left(\dfrac{|u|^{p}}{R^{p}}\omega^{p}\right)^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}\\
&\quad+c(\sigma)\big(\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{p-1}}+C^{*}(\varepsilon)\big)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2\Omega_{r}}(|F|^{p}\omega^{p})^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}\\
&=I_{5}+I_{6}+I_{7}+I_{8}.\end{split}

By Lemma
2.7
and (
3.1
),
\varphi_{|\nabla z|}(|\nabla z-\nabla h|)\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}\eqsim|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla h)-\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla z)|^{2}
. Then by choosing
\sigma\in(0,1)
sufficiently small depending on
n,p
and
\Lambda
,
I_{5}
is absorbed to the left-hand side. Finally,
\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{p-1}}\leq C^{*}(\varepsilon)
holds when
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
, and so the estimate (
3.22
) holds true.
∎

Now, we give the second comparison estimate. With
y=\Psi(x)
, we define

(3.40)

\displaystyle\tilde{h}(y):=h(\Psi^{-1}(y)).

Let
\tilde{v}=\tilde{v}(y)\in W^{1,p}(\Psi(\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}))
be the weak solution of

(3.41)

\displaystyle\begin{split}-\operatorname{div}_{y}(|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\nabla_{y}\tilde{v}|^{p-2}\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}^{2}\nabla_{y}\tilde{v})&=0\quad\text{in }\Psi(\tfrac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}),\\
\tilde{v}&=\tilde{h}\quad\text{on }\partial(\Psi(\tfrac{1}{2}\Omega_{r})).\end{split}

Since
\Psi
is a homeomorphism, together with (
1.14
) we have
\partial(\Psi(\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}))=\Psi(\partial(\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}))
. Denoting
v(x)=\tilde{v}(y)=\tilde{v}(\Psi(x))
, we have

(3.42)

\displaystyle\nabla v(x)=\nabla\tilde{v}(\Psi(x))=(\nabla\Psi)(x)\nabla_{y}\tilde{v}(\Psi(x))=(\nabla\Psi)(x)\nabla_{y}\tilde{v}(y).

Denoting
T(x)=(\nabla\Psi)^{-1}(x)
, (
3.41
) transforms into

(3.43)

\displaystyle\begin{split}-\operatorname{div}(T^{t}(x)|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\nabla&v|^{p-2}\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}^{2}T(x)\nabla v)=0\quad\text{in }\tfrac{1}{2}\Omega_{r},\\
v(x)&=h(x)\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\,\text{on }\partial(\tfrac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}),\end{split}

where
T^{t}(x)
abbreviation stands for transpose. Or equivalently, denoting

(3.44)

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{\Psi}(x,\xi):=T^{t}(x)|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\xi|^{p-2}\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}^{2}T(x)\xi,

we have

(3.45)

\displaystyle\begin{split}-\operatorname{div}(\mathcal{A}_{\Psi}(x,\nabla v))&=0\quad\quad\,\,\text{in }\tfrac{1}{2}\Omega_{r},\\
v(x)&=h(x)\quad\text{on }\partial(\tfrac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}).\end{split}

The problems (
3.43
) and (
3.45
) can be derived also from the weak formulation of the equation. At this time we have also used that
\det(\nabla\Psi)=1
for the change of coordinate in the integrals.
The natural function space for
v
is
W^{1,p}_{\omega_{B_{r}}}(\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r})
and
v
is the unique minimizer of

(3.46)

\displaystyle w\mapsto\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}\varphi(|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\nabla w|)\,dx

subject to the boundary condition
v=h
on
\partial(\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r})
.

Now, we need the following lemma.

Lemma 3.6
.

Assume (
2.4
). For all
\xi\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
and
x\in\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}
we have

(3.47)

\displaystyle|T(x)|\eqsim|T^{-1}(x)|\eqsim c

and

(3.48)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{A}_{B}(\xi)-\mathcal{A}_{\Psi}(x,\xi)|\leq c\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}\omega_{B}^{p}\min\left\{|\xi|^{p-1},|T(x)\xi|^{p-1}\right\}

for some
c=c(n,p,\Lambda)
.

Proof.

First, since
(T(x)-\mathrm{Id})^{2}=((\nabla\Psi(x))^{-1}-\mathrm{Id})^{2}=0
, we have
T^{-1}(x)-\mathrm{Id}=\mathrm{Id}-T(x)
. Then

(3.49)

\displaystyle|T^{-1}(x)-\mathrm{Id}|=|\mathrm{Id}-T(x)|\leq n\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}

holds, and so together with
\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{1}{2n}
, it follows that

(3.50)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\leq|\mathrm{Id}|-n\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}\leq|T(x)|\leq|\mathrm{Id}|+n\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{3}{2}.

Hence we have
|T(x)|\eqsim c
. Similarly, we have
|T^{-1}(x)|\eqsim c
.

On the other hand, observe that by (
3.15
),

\displaystyle|\mathcal{A}_{B}(\xi)-\mathcal{A}_{\Psi}(x,\xi)|

\displaystyle=\left||\mathbb{M}_{B}\xi|^{p-2}\mathbb{M}^{2}_{B}\xi-|\mathbb{M}_{B}T(x)\xi|^{p-2}T^{t}(x)\mathbb{M}^{2}_{B}T(x)\xi\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathbb{M}_{B}|\mathbb{M}_{B}\xi|^{p-2}\mathbb{M}_{B}\xi-\mathbb{M}_{B}|\mathbb{M}_{B}T(x)\xi|^{p-2}\mathbb{M}_{B}T(x)\xi\right|

\displaystyle\quad+|\mathbb{M}_{B}-T^{t}(x)\mathbb{M}_{B}|\cdot\left||\mathbb{M}_{B}T(x)\xi|^{p-2}\mathbb{M}_{B}T(x)\xi\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\omega_{B}\cdot|A(\mathbb{M}_{B}\xi)-A(\mathbb{M}_{B}T(x)\xi)|+\omega_{B}\cdot|\mathrm{Id}-T^{t}(x)|\cdot|\mathbb{M}_{B}T(x)\xi|^{p-1}.

Here, by (
3.47
) and (
3.15
),

\displaystyle|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\xi|=|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T^{-1}(x)T(x)\xi|\lesssim|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}|\cdot|T^{-1}(x)|\cdot|T(x)\xi|\lesssim|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}|\cdot|T(x)\xi|\lesssim|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\xi|

and similarly
|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\xi|\lesssim|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)T^{-1}(x)\xi|=|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\xi|
holds. Thus we have
|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\xi|\eqsim|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\xi|
. Then together with (
3.49
) and Lemma
2.7
, there holds

\displaystyle|A(\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\xi)

\displaystyle-A(\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\xi)|\eqsim\varphi^{\prime}_{|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\xi|\vee|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\xi|}\left(|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\xi-\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\xi|\right)

\displaystyle\eqsim\left(|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\xi|\vee|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\xi|\vee|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\xi-\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\xi|\right)^{p-2}|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\xi-\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\xi|

\displaystyle\lesssim\omega_{B_{r}}|\mathrm{Id}-T(x)||\xi|\left(|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\xi|\vee|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\xi|\vee|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\xi-\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\xi|\right)^{p-2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\omega_{B_{r}}|\mathrm{Id}-T(x)||\xi|\dfrac{\left(|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\xi|+|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\xi|\right)^{p-1}}{|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\xi|}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Lambda|\mathrm{Id}-T(x)|\left(|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\xi|+|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\xi|\right)^{p-1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\xi|^{p-1}.

Thus, together with
|\mathrm{Id}-T^{t}(x)|=|(\mathrm{Id}-T(x))^{t}|=|\mathrm{Id}-T(x)|\leq n\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}
, we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{A}_{B_{r}}(\xi)-\mathcal{A}_{\Psi}(x,\xi)|\lesssim\omega_{B_{r}}\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\xi|^{p-1}\lesssim\omega_{B_{r}}^{p}\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}|T(x)\xi|^{p-1}.

Finally, since
\omega_{B_{r}}|T(x)\xi|\eqsim|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\xi|\eqsim|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\xi|\eqsim\omega_{B_{r}}|\xi|
, we get the conclusion.
∎

Now, we can compute the comparison estimate.

Proposition 3.7

(Second comparison at boundary)
.

Assuming (
2.4
), let
v
be as in (
3.43
) and
h
be as in (
3.19
). There exists
\kappa_{4}=\kappa_{4}(n,p,\Lambda)
such that if
\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(4B_{r})}\leq\kappa_{4}
, then for some
c=c(n,p,\Lambda)
, we have

(3.51)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}&|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla v)-\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla h)|^{2}\,dx\leq c\|\nabla\psi\|^{2}_{\infty}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}(|\nabla h|^{p}\omega_{B_{r}}^{p})\,dx.\end{split}

Proof.

Test
v-h
to (
3.19
) and (
3.43
) to have

(3.52)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}&\left(\mathcal{A}_{B_{r}}(\nabla v)-\mathcal{A}_{B_{r}}(\nabla h)\right)\cdot(\nabla v-\nabla h)\,dx\\
&=\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}\left(\mathcal{A}_{B_{r}}(\nabla v)-\mathcal{A}_{\Psi}(x,\nabla v)\right)\cdot(\nabla v-\nabla h)\,dx.\end{split}

We apply Lemma
2.7
, (
3.48
) and then use Young’s inequality to obtain

(3.53)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla v)-\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla h)|^{2}\,dx&\eqsim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}\varphi_{|\nabla v|}(|\nabla v-\nabla h|)\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{A}_{\Psi}(x,\nabla v)-\mathcal{A}_{B_{r}}(\nabla v)||\nabla v-\nabla h|\,dx\\
&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}\omega_{B_{r}}^{p}\varphi^{\prime}(|\nabla v|)|\nabla v-\nabla h|\,dx\\
&\leq\sigma\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}\varphi_{|\nabla v|}(|\nabla v-\nabla h|)\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}\,dx\\
&\quad+c(\sigma)\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}(\varphi_{|\nabla v|})^{*}(\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}\varphi^{\prime}(|\nabla v|))\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}\,dx=:I_{1}+I_{2}\end{split}

for any
\sigma\in(0,1)
. Then
I_{1}
is absorbed to the left-hand side by choosing
\sigma
sufficiently small depending on
n,p
and
\Lambda
. To estimate
I_{2}
, we use (
2.19
),
(\varphi^{*})_{\varphi^{\prime}(|a|)}(\varphi^{\prime}(|a|))\eqsim\varphi(|a|)
and then (
3.47
) to have

(3.54)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{2}&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}(\varphi^{*})_{\varphi^{\prime}(|\nabla v|)}(\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}\varphi^{\prime}(|\nabla v|))\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}^{2}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}(\varphi^{*})_{\varphi^{\prime}(|\nabla v|)}(\varphi^{\prime}(|\nabla v|))\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}^{2}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}\varphi(|\nabla v|)\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}^{2}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}\varphi(|T(x)\nabla v|)\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}\,dx.\end{split}

Now, we use minimizing property of
\nabla v
together with (
3.15
), (
3.47
) and
|\Omega_{r}|\eqsim|\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}|
to have

(3.55)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}\varphi(|T(x)\nabla v|)\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}\,dx&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}\varphi(|T(x)\nabla h|)\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}\,dx\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}\varphi(|\nabla h|)\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}\,dx.\end{split}

Summing up the above estimates, we obtain (
3.51
).
∎

3.3.
Decay Estimates at Boundary

Before providing decay estimates of
\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)
, we discuss some regularity results and corresponding estimates related to
\tilde{v}
and
v
which are defined in (
3.41
) and (
3.45
), respectively. First, we have the following estimates which imply Lipschitz regularity and
C^{1,\alpha}
regularity of
\tilde{v}
.

Proposition 3.8
.

Assuming (
2.4
), let
\tilde{v}
be the solution of (
3.19
). Then there holds

(3.56)

\displaystyle\sup_{\frac{1}{32}B_{r}\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|\nabla\tilde{v}|^{p}\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}\leq c\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}B_{r}\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|\nabla\tilde{v}|^{p}\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}\,dy.

Moreover, there exist
\alpha=\alpha(n,p,\Lambda)\in(0,1)
and
c=c(n,p,\Lambda)>0
such that

(3.57)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\lambda B_{r}\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\lambda{B_{r}}\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|^{2}\,dy\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\leq c\lambda^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{8}{B_{r}}\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\frac{1}{8}B_{r}\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|^{2}\,dy\end{split}

holds for all
\lambda\in(0,\frac{1}{80})
.

Proof.

We first show (
3.57
). Throughout the proof of (
3.57
), let us write the center of the ball
B_{r}
as
y_{B_{r}}
.

Step 1.

If
\frac{1}{8}B_{r}\subset\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n}:y_{n}\geq 0\}
, then it directly follows from
[
BDGN21
, Proposition 15]
.

Step 2.

Now, we consider
z_{B_{r}}\in\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n}:y_{n}=0\}
. We have by (
3.16
) that

\displaystyle\Lambda^{-1}\omega_{B_{r}}\mathrm{Id}\leq\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\leq\omega_{B_{r}}\mathrm{Id}.

Since the equation (
3.41
) and estimate (
3.57
) are invariant under normalization, without loss of generality we let
\omega_{B_{r}}=1
. Also, assume that
{B_{r}}
is centered at 0, i.e.,
z_{B_{r}}=0
.

Since
\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}
is symmetric, there is an orthogonal matrix
Q
and a diagonal matrix
\mathbb{D}_{B_{r}}
such that
\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}=Q\mathbb{D}_{B_{r}}Q^{*}
. Then
\tilde{w}_{0}(y):=\tilde{v}(Qy)
is a solution of (
3.41
) with
\mathbb{D}_{B_{r}}
instead of
\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}
. Notice that the boundary of the domain is also rotated, and for
\mathbb{D}_{B_{r}}
we have

(3.58)

\displaystyle\Lambda^{-1}\mathrm{Id}\leq\mathbb{D}_{B_{r}}\leq\mathrm{Id}.

Now, we apply an anisotropic scaling
y\mapsto\mathbb{D}^{-1}_{{B_{r}}}y
. This turns estimates on half balls (with the rotated flat part) into estimates on half-ellipses (with the rotated flat part) of uniformly bounded eccentricity depending on
n
and
\Lambda
. Thus, after properly rotating the coordinate axis to make the rotated flat part to the subset of
\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n}:y_{n}=0\}
, we can take the odd extension to (
3.41
) to obtain (
3.57
).

In detail, let
\tilde{Q}
be an
n\times n
orthogonal matrix which maps
\mathbb{D}^{-1}_{{B_{r}}}Q^{*}(\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n}:y_{n}=0\})
to
\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n}:y_{n}=0\}
. In other words,
\tilde{Q}
satisfies

(3.59)

\displaystyle\tilde{Q}(\mathbb{D}^{-1}_{{B_{r}}}Q^{*}(\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n}:y_{n}=0\}))=\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n}:y_{n}=0\}.

Now, using this
n\times n
orthogonal matrix
\tilde{Q}
, define

(3.60)

\displaystyle\tilde{w}(y):=\tilde{v}(\tilde{Q}\mathbb{D}_{B_{r}}Q^{*}y).

Then we have

(3.61)

\displaystyle(Q\mathbb{D}_{B_{r}}^{-1}(\tilde{Q})^{*})^{*}\mathbb{M}_{{B_{r}}}^{2}(Q\mathbb{D}_{B_{r}}^{-1}(\tilde{Q})^{*})=\tilde{Q}\mathbb{D}^{-1}_{{B_{r}}}Q^{*}\mathbb{M}^{2}_{{B_{r}}}Q\mathbb{D}^{-1}_{B_{r}}(\tilde{Q})^{*}=\tilde{Q}\mathbb{D}^{-1}_{{B_{r}}}\mathbb{D}_{{B_{r}}}^{2}\mathbb{D}^{-1}_{B_{r}}(\tilde{Q})^{*}=\mathrm{Id}

and for
t=Q\mathbb{D}^{-1}_{B_{r}}(\tilde{Q})^{*}y
, we have

(3.62)

\displaystyle\tilde{w}(Q\mathbb{D}^{-1}_{B_{r}}(\tilde{Q})^{*}y)=\tilde{v}(y)=\tilde{w}(t)

and so

(3.63)

\displaystyle\nabla_{y}\tilde{v}(y)=\nabla_{y}\tilde{w}(t)=(Q\mathbb{D}^{-1}_{{B_{r}}}(\tilde{Q})^{*})\nabla_{t}\tilde{w}(t).

Therefore, (
3.41
) defined in
\Psi(\tfrac{1}{2}\Omega_{r})\subset{B_{r}}
transforms into

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\operatorname{div}_{y}(|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\nabla\tilde{v}|^{p-2}\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}^{2}\nabla\tilde{v})=\operatorname{div}_{y}(\left<\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\nabla\tilde{v},\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\nabla\tilde{v}\right>^{\frac{p-2}{2}}\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}^{2}\nabla\tilde{v})\\
&=\operatorname{div}_{t}\left((Q\mathbb{D}^{-1}_{B_{r}}(\tilde{Q})^{*})^{*}\left<\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}Q\mathbb{D}^{-1}_{B_{r}}(\tilde{Q})^{*}\nabla_{t}\tilde{w},\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}Q\mathbb{D}^{-1}_{B_{r}}(\tilde{Q})^{*}\nabla_{t}\tilde{w}\right>^{\frac{p-2}{2}}\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}^{2}Q\mathbb{D}^{-1}_{{B_{r}}}(\tilde{Q})^{*}\nabla_{t}\tilde{w}\right)\\
&=\operatorname{div}_{t}\left(\left<(\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}Q\mathbb{D}^{-1}_{B_{r}}(\tilde{Q})^{*})^{*}\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}Q\mathbb{D}^{-1}_{B_{r}}(\tilde{Q})^{*}\nabla_{t}\tilde{w},\nabla_{t}\tilde{w}\right>^{\frac{p-2}{2}}\nabla_{t}\tilde{w}\right)\\
&=\operatorname{div}_{t}(|\nabla_{t}\tilde{w}|^{p-2}\nabla_{t}\tilde{w})=0\end{split}

defined in
t\in Q\mathbb{D}^{-1}_{B_{r}}(\tilde{Q})^{*}(\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n}:y_{n}=0\})
. Note that since
\Psi(\tfrac{1}{2}\Omega_{r})\subset\{y\in\mathbb{R}:y_{n}=0\}
and (
3.59
) hold, we can employ
[
Mar81
]
and apply the odd extension for
\operatorname{div}_{t}(|\nabla_{t}\tilde{w}|^{p-2}\nabla_{t}\tilde{w})=0
and get the analogous estimate to (
3.57
) for
w
with half-ellipses instead of half-balls. Using the relation (
3.61
) and
\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}=Q\mathbb{D}_{B_{r}}Q^{*}
for changing
w
to
v
, and then using the fact that all balls can be covered by slightly enlarged ellipses and vice versa, the estimate (
3.57
) is also true for half balls. Then we have (
3.57
).

Step 3.

Now, we consider the general case, i.e.,
\frac{1}{4}{B_{r}}\not\subset\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n}:y_{n}\geq 0\}
and
z=z_{B_{r}}\not\in\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n}:y_{n}=0\}
holds. We employ the argument of
[
KZ01
, Lemma 3.7]
. Denote
z=(z_{1},\dots,z_{n-1},z_{n})
,
\bar{z}=(z_{1},\dots,z_{n-1},0)
, and recall that
0<\lambda<\frac{1}{80}
and
z_{n}>0
since
\tilde{x}\in\Omega
, where
\tilde{x}
is the center of the ball
B_{r}
with
4B_{r}\subset 2B_{0}
.
Let us specify the exact center of the balls in this step. In particular, we write
{B_{r}}={B_{r}}(z)
.

Case 1.

z_{n}>\tfrac{r}{10}
. In this case we have

(3.64)

\displaystyle\lambda{B_{r}}(z)\subset\tfrac{1}{10}B_{r}(z)\subset\tfrac{z_{n}}{r}B_{r}(z)\subset\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}.

By the interior estimates in
[
DSV09
, Theorem 6.4]
, we obtain

(3.65)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{{\lambda B_{r}}(z)}&|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\lambda B_{r}(z)}|^{2}\,dy\\
&\lesssim\left(\dfrac{\lambda}{1/10}\right)^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{10}B_{r}(z)}|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\frac{1}{10}B_{r}(z)}|^{2}\,dy\\
&\lesssim\left(\frac{\lambda}{1/10}\right)^{2\alpha}\left(\dfrac{1}{|\frac{1}{10}B_{r}(z)|}\int_{\frac{1}{4}B_{r}(z)\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\frac{1}{4}B_{r}(z)\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|^{2}\,dy\right)\\
&\lesssim\lambda^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}B_{r}(z)\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\frac{1}{4}B_{r}(z)\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|^{2}\,dy.\end{split}

Thus we obtain (
3.57
) in this case.

Case 2.

0<z_{n}\leq\frac{r}{10}
. We divide the proof into two subcases.

Subcase 1.

0<\lambda<\frac{z_{n}}{4r}
. In this subcase

(3.66)

\displaystyle\lambda B_{r}(z)\subset\tfrac{z_{n}}{4r}B_{r}(z)\subset\tfrac{5z_{n}}{4r}B^{+}_{r}(\bar{z}).

By the interior estimates in
[
DSV09
, Theorem 6.4]
, we have

(3.67)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\lambda B_{r}(z)}&|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\lambda B_{r}(z)}|^{2}\,dy\\
&\lesssim\left(\dfrac{4\lambda}{z_{n}/4r}\right)^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{z_{n}}{4r}B_{r}(z)}|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\frac{z_{n}}{4r}B_{r}(z)}|^{2}\,dy\\
&\lesssim\left(\dfrac{4\lambda}{z_{n}/4r}\right)^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{5z_{n}}{4r}B^{+}_{r}(\bar{z})}|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\frac{5z_{n}}{4r}B^{+}_{r}(\bar{z})}|^{2}\,dy.\end{split}

Since
\bar{z}\in\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n}:z_{n}=0\}
, by
Step 2
above and then using
0<z_{n}\leq\frac{r}{10}
, we have

(3.68)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{5z_{n}}{4r}B^{+}_{r}(\bar{z})}&|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\frac{5z_{n}}{4r}B^{+}_{r}(\bar{z})}|^{2}\,dy\\
&\lesssim\left(\dfrac{5z_{n}}{r}\right)^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{8}B^{+}_{r}(\bar{z})}|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\frac{1}{8}B^{+}_{r}(\bar{z})}|^{2}\,dy\\
&\lesssim\left(\dfrac{5z_{n}}{r}\right)^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}B_{r}(z)\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\frac{1}{4}B_{r}(z)\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|^{2}\,dy.\end{split}

Combining the above two estimates, (
3.67
) and (
3.68
), we obtain (
3.57
) in this subcase.

Subcase 2.

\lambda\geq\tfrac{z_{n}}{4r}
. Since
\lambda\leq\frac{1}{40}
, we see that

(3.69)

\displaystyle\lambda B_{r}(z)\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}\subset 5\lambda B^{+}_{r}(\bar{z})\subset\tfrac{1}{8}B^{+}_{r}(\bar{z})\subset\tfrac{1}{4}B_{r}(z)\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}.

Therefore, using the boundary estimate above in
Step 2
, we have

(3.70)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\lambda B_{r}(z)\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}&|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\lambda B_{r}(z)\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|^{2}\,dy\\
&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{20\lambda B^{+}_{r}(\bar{z})}|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{20\lambda B^{+}_{r}(\bar{z})}|^{2}\,dy\\
&\lesssim\left(\dfrac{5\lambda}{1/8}\right)^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{8}B^{+}_{r}(\bar{z})}|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\frac{1}{8}B^{+}_{r}(\bar{z})}|^{2}\,dy\\
&\lesssim\lambda^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}B_{r}(z)\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\frac{1}{4}B_{r}(z)\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|^{2}\,dy.\end{split}

Merging all cases
Case 1
–
Case 2
, we have (
3.57
) in
Step 3
. Therefore, by
Step 1
–
Step 3
, we have (
3.57
).

To show (
3.56
), we employ the similar argument as above. If
\frac{1}{4}{B_{r}}\subset\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n}:y_{n}\geq 0\}
, then it follows from
[
BDGN21
, Proposition 15]
. Now, when
z_{{B_{r}}}\in\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n}:y_{n}=0\}
, by employing the same matrix
\tilde{Q}
,
\mathbb{D}_{B_{r}}
,
Q^{*}
as above, we can apply
[
Lie88
, Lemma 5]
and we have (
3.56
) in this case. In the general case, i.e., when
\frac{1}{4}{B_{r}}\not\subset\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n}:y_{n}\geq 0\}
and
z_{B_{r}}\not\in\{y\in\mathbb{R}^{n}:y_{n}=0\}
holds, we divide the cases as same as above and apply the argument of
[
Lie91
]
instead of
[
DSV09
]
. Now, (
3.56
) is obtained.
∎

Now, we transform the above estimates for
\tilde{v}
to the estimates for
v
.

Proposition 3.9
.

Assuming (
2.4
), let
v
be the solution of (
3.45
). Then there holds

(3.71)

\displaystyle\sup_{\frac{1}{32}\Omega_{r}}|\nabla v|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\leq c\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}|\nabla v|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\,dx.

Moreover, there exist
\alpha=\alpha(n,p,\Lambda)\in(0,1)
and
c=c(n,p,\Lambda)>0
such that

(3.72)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\lambda\Omega_{r}}&|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla v)-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla v)}\rangle_{\lambda\Omega_{r}}|^{2}\,dx\\
&\leq c\lambda^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla v)-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla v)}\rangle_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}|^{2}\,dx+c\|\nabla\psi\|^{2}_{\infty}\lambda^{-n}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}|\nabla v|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\,dx\end{split}

holds for all
\lambda\in(0,\frac{1}{80})
.

Proof.

To obtain (
3.72
), using (
2.5
) with the help of (
2.4
), there holds

(3.73)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\lambda\Omega_{r}}&|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla v)-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla v)}\rangle_{\lambda\Omega_{r}}|^{2}\,dx\\
&=\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Psi(\lambda\Omega_{r})}|\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}((\nabla\Psi)\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}((\nabla\Psi)\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\Psi(\lambda\Omega_{r})}|^{2}\,dy\\
&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Psi(4\lambda\Omega_{r})}|\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}((\nabla\Psi)\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\Psi(4\lambda\Omega_{r})}|^{2}\,dy\\
&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Psi(4\lambda\Omega_{r})}|\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}((\nabla\Psi)\nabla\tilde{v})-\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla\tilde{v})|^{2}\,dy\\
&\quad+\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Psi(4\lambda\Omega_{r})}|\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\Psi(4\lambda\Omega_{r})}|^{2}\,dy=:I_{1}+I_{2}.\end{split}

For
I_{1}
, by Lemma
2.7
and Lemma
3.6
, we obtain

(3.74)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{1}&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Psi(4\lambda\Omega_{r})}|\mathcal{A}((\nabla\Psi)\nabla\tilde{v})-\mathcal{A}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})|\cdot|(\nabla\Psi)\nabla\tilde{v}-\nabla\tilde{v}|\,dy\\
&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Psi(4\lambda\Omega_{r})}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}|(\nabla\Psi)\nabla\tilde{v}|^{p-1}\cdot\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}|\nabla\tilde{v}|\,dy\\
&\lesssim\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}^{2}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Psi(4\lambda\Omega_{r})}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}|(\nabla\Psi)\nabla\tilde{v}|^{p}|(\nabla\Psi)^{-1}|\,dy\\
&\lesssim\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}^{2}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{4\lambda\Omega_{r}}|\nabla v|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}^{2}\lambda^{-n}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}|\nabla v|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\,dx.\end{split}

On the other hand, for
I_{2}
, we apply (
2.5
) from (
2.4
), and (
3.57
) to have

(3.75)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{2}&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{8\lambda{B_{r}}\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{8\lambda{B_{r}}\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|^{2}\,dy\\
&\lesssim\lambda^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{8}{B_{r}}\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla\tilde{v})-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla\tilde{v})}\rangle_{\frac{1}{8}{B_{r}}\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|^{2}\,dy\\
&\lesssim\lambda^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Psi^{-1}(\frac{1}{8}{B_{r}}\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+})}|\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}((\nabla\Psi)^{-1}\nabla v)-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla v)}\rangle_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}|^{2}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\lambda^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}((\nabla\Psi)^{-1}\nabla v)-\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla v)|^{2}\,dx\\
&\quad+\lambda^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla v)-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla v)}\rangle_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}|^{2}\,dx\\
&=:\lambda^{2\alpha}(I_{2,1}+I_{2,2}).\end{split}

To obtain (
3.72
), we only have to estimate
I_{2,1}
. By the similar argument as (
3.74
), we have

(3.76)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{2,1}&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{A}((\nabla\Psi)^{-1}\nabla v)-\mathcal{A}_{B_{r}}(\nabla v)|\cdot\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}|\nabla v|\,dx\\
&\lesssim\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}^{2}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}|(\nabla\Psi)^{-1}\nabla v|^{p}|\nabla\Psi|\,dx\\
&\lesssim\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}^{2}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}|\nabla v|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\,dx.\end{split}

Summing up all estimates (
3.73
)–(
3.76
), we conclude (
3.72
).

To show (
3.71
), by using (
3.47
) and (
2.5
), there holds

(3.77)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sup_{x\in\frac{1}{32}\Omega_{r}}|\nabla v(x)|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\leq\sup_{y\in\Psi(\frac{1}{32}\Omega_{r})}|(\nabla\Psi)\nabla\tilde{v}(y)|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\lesssim\sup_{y\in\frac{1}{16}{B_{r}}\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|\nabla\tilde{v}(y)|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\end{split}

and by (
3.56
), we have

(3.78)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sup_{y\in\frac{1}{16}{B_{r}}\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|\nabla\tilde{v}(y)|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{8}{B_{r}}\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}|\nabla\tilde{v}|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\,dy\\
&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Psi^{-1}(\frac{1}{8}{B_{r}}\cap\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+})}|(\nabla\Psi)^{-1}\nabla v|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}|\nabla v|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\,dx.\end{split}

Summing up the above two inequalities, we have (
3.71
).
∎

With the help of the above estimates for
v
, we give the following decay estimate of
\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)
. Recall that for
B_{0}=B_{R}(x_{0})
with
x_{0}\in\partial\Omega
, let
B_{r}=B_{r}(\tilde{x})
with
\tilde{x}\in\overline{\Omega}
and
4{B_{r}}\subset 2B_{0}
. Also,
z
on
B_{0}\cap\Omega
is such that
z:=u\eta^{p^{\prime}}
and we take the zero extension for
z
on
\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus(B_{0}\cap\Omega)
, if necessary.

Proposition 3.10

(Decay estimate at boundary)
.

Let
z
,
u
and
F
be as in (
3.5
) and (
3.9
). There exist
\lambda=\lambda(n,p,\Lambda)\in(0,\frac{1}{80})
,
s=s(n,p,\Lambda)>1
and
\kappa_{5}=\kappa_{5}(n,p,\Lambda)\in(0,1)
such that the following holds: If
\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(4{B_{r}})}\leq\kappa_{5}
, and (
2.4
) hold, then for every
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
there holds

(3.79)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\lambda{B_{r}}}&|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)-\langle{\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)}\rangle_{\lambda{B_{r}}}|^{2}\,dx\\
&\qquad\leq\dfrac{1}{4}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{{B_{r}}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)-\langle{\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)}\rangle_{{B_{r}}}|^{2}\,dx\\
&\qquad\quad+c\left(\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|^{2}_{{\mathrm{BMO}}({B_{r}})}+\|\nabla\psi\|^{2}_{\infty}+\varepsilon\right)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{{B_{r}}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)|^{2s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}\\
&\qquad\quad+c\,C^{*}(\varepsilon)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2{B_{r}}}\left(\dfrac{\chi_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}|u|^{p}}{R^{p}}\omega^{p}\right)^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}\\
&\qquad\quad+c\,C^{*}(\varepsilon)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2{B_{r}}}|\chi_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}\mathcal{V}(x,F)|^{2s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}\end{split}

with
c=c(n,p,\Lambda)
and
C^{*}(\varepsilon)
defined in (
3.34
).

Proof.

We first assume
\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(4{B_{r}})}\leq\kappa_{4}
with
\kappa_{4}
from Proposition
3.7
. Since
z=|\nabla z|=0
in
\lambda{B_{r}}\setminus\Omega
and
|\lambda\Omega_{r}|\eqsim|\lambda{B_{r}}|
hold from (
2.6
) because of (
2.4
) and
\tilde{x}\in\overline{\Omega}
, we have

(3.80)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{1}&:=\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\lambda{B_{r}}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)-\langle{\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)}\rangle_{\lambda{B_{r}}}|^{2}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\lambda{B_{r}}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)-\langle{\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla v)}\rangle_{\lambda\Omega_{r}}|^{2}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\lambda\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)-\langle{\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla v)}\rangle_{\lambda\Omega_{r}}|^{2}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\lambda\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla v)-\langle{\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla v)}\rangle_{\lambda\Omega_{r}}|^{2}\,dx+\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\lambda\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)-\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla v)|^{2}\,dx\\
&=:I_{2}+I_{3}.\end{split}

We start to estimate
I_{3}
. There holds

(3.81)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{3}&\lesssim\lambda^{-n}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)-\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla v)|^{2}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\lambda^{-n}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla z)-\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla v)|^{2}\,dx\\
