&\quad+\lambda^{-n}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)-\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla z)|^{2}\,dx+\lambda^{-n}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{32}\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla v)-\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla v)|^{2}\,dx\\
&=:I_{3,1}+I_{3,2}+I_{3,3}.\end{split}

For
I_{3,2}
and
I_{3,3}
, since
\tilde{x}\in\overline{\Omega}
and (
2.4
) holds,
|\Omega_{r}|\eqsim|{B_{r}}|
also holds. Then we can apply the similar argument of the proof as in Proposition 18 in
[
BDGN21
]
. By (
3.1
), (
3.15
), (
3.16
), Lemma
2.7
, (
2.19
), and Hölder’s inequality with exponents
(2s^{\prime},s,2s^{\prime})
, we have

(3.82)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{3,2}&\lesssim\lambda^{-n}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}\left(\dfrac{|\mathbb{M}_{{B_{r}}}-\mathbb{M}|}{|\mathbb{M}_{{B_{r}}}|}\right)^{4s^{\prime}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2s^{\prime}}}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}(|\nabla z|^{p}\omega^{p})^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}\\
&\quad\quad\times\left(1+\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}\left(\dfrac{\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}}{\omega^{p}}\right)^{2s^{\prime}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2s^{\prime}}}\right).\end{split}

Together with
z=|\nabla z|=0
in
{B_{r}}\setminus\Omega
and
|\Omega_{r}|\eqsim|{B_{r}}|
due to (
2.4
), we obtain

(3.83)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{3,2}\lesssim\lambda^{-n}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}(|\nabla z|^{p}\omega^{p})^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}.\end{split}

Note that by
4{B_{r}}\subset 2B_{0}
,
\chi_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}
can be inserted in the integrand of the above estimate. On the other hand, similar to
I_{3,2}
, we continue to estimate
I_{3,3}
as follows:

(3.84)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{3,3}&=\lambda^{-n}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{32}\Omega_{r}}|V(\mathbb{M}\nabla v)-V(\mathbb{M}_{{B_{r}}}\nabla v)|^{2}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\lambda^{-n}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{32}\Omega_{r}}\left(\dfrac{|\mathbb{M}-\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}|}{|\mathbb{M}_{{B_{r}}}|}\right)^{2}\left(|\nabla v|^{p}\omega^{p}+|\nabla v|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\right)\,dx\\
&\lesssim\lambda^{-n}\left(\sup_{\frac{1}{32}\Omega_{r}}|\nabla v|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\right)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}\left(\dfrac{|\mathbb{M}-\mathbb{M}_{{B_{r}}}|}{|\mathbb{M}_{{B_{r}}}|}\right)^{2}\left(\dfrac{\omega^{p}}{\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}}+1\right)\,dx\right)=\lambda^{-n}I_{3,3,1}I_{3,3,2}.\end{split}

Here, (
3.71
) in Proposition
3.9
, (
3.47
) and (
3.15
) imply

(3.85)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{3,3,1}&:=\sup_{\frac{1}{32}\Omega_{r}}|\nabla v|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\\
&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}|\nabla v|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\,dx\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\nabla v|^{p}|T^{-1}(x)|^{p}\,dx\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\nabla v|^{p}\,dx\end{split}

and the minimizing property of
v
and
h
, together with (
3.47
) and (
3.15
) give us that

(3.86)

\displaystyle I_{3,3,1}\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}T(x)\nabla h|^{p}\,dx\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}|\mathbb{M}_{B_{r}}\nabla h|^{p}\,dx\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}|\nabla z|^{p}\omega_{B_{r}}^{p}\,dx.

Then we use Hölder’s inequality,
|\Omega_{r}|\eqsim|{B_{r}}|
from (
2.4
), Lemma
2.5
and
z=|\nabla z|=0
in
{B_{r}}\setminus\Omega
to obtain

(3.87)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{3,3,1}\lesssim\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}(|\nabla z|^{p}\omega^{p})^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}\left(\dfrac{\omega^{p}_{B_{r}}}{\omega^{p}}\right)^{\frac{s}{s-1}}\,dx\right)^{1-\frac{1}{s}}\lesssim\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{{B_{r}}}(|\nabla z|^{p}\omega^{p})^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}},\end{split}

provided
\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(4{B_{r}})}\leq\kappa_{4}
holds.
On the other hand, by Hölder’s inequality, Lemma
2.4
and Lemma
2.5
we obtain

(3.88)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{3,3,2}&\lesssim\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}\left(\dfrac{|\mathbb{M}-\mathbb{M}_{{B_{r}}}|}{|\mathbb{M}_{{B_{r}}}|}\right)^{4}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}\left(\dfrac{\omega^{p}}{\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}}\right)^{2}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+1\right)\lesssim\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|^{2}_{{\mathrm{BMO}}({B_{r}})}.\end{split}

Summing up, there holds

(3.89)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{3,3}&\lesssim\lambda^{-n}\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|^{2}_{{\mathrm{BMO}}({B_{r}})}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{{B_{r}}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)|^{2s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}.\end{split}

For
I_{3,1}
, we first apply Proposition
3.5
and Proposition
3.7
, use
z=|\nabla z|=0
in
2{B_{r}}\setminus\Omega
and
|2\Omega_{r}|\eqsim|2{B_{r}}|
with the help of (
2.4
), and argue similarly to (
3.86
)–(
3.87
) for the integral of
|\nabla h|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}
term. The resulting estimate is as follows:

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{3,1}&\lesssim\lambda^{-n}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla z)-\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla h)|^{2}\,dx+\lambda^{-n}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla h)-\mathcal{V}_{B_{r}}(\nabla v)|^{2}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\lambda^{-n}(\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|^{2}_{{\mathrm{BMO}}({B_{r}})}+\varepsilon)\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}(|\nabla z|^{p}\omega^{p})\,dx+\lambda^{-n}\|\nabla\psi\|^{2}_{\infty}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}(|\nabla h|^{p}\omega_{B_{r}}^{p})\,dx\\
&\quad+\lambda^{-n}C^{*}(\varepsilon)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2\Omega_{r}}\left(\dfrac{|u|^{p}}{R^{p}}\omega^{p}\right)^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}+\lambda^{-n}C^{*}(\varepsilon)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}(x,F)|^{2s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}\\
&\lesssim\lambda^{-n}(\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|^{2}_{{\mathrm{BMO}}({B_{r}})}+\|\nabla\psi\|^{2}_{\infty}+\varepsilon)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{{B_{r}}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)|^{2s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}\\
&\quad+\lambda^{-n}C^{*}(\varepsilon)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2{B_{r}}}\left(\dfrac{\chi_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}|u|^{p}}{R^{p}}\omega^{p}\right)^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}+\lambda^{-n}C^{*}(\varepsilon)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2{B_{r}}}|\chi_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}\mathcal{V}(x,F)|^{2s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}.\end{split}

Consequently, we have

(3.90)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{3}&\lesssim\lambda^{-n}\left(\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|^{2}_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(4{B_{r}})}+\|\nabla\psi\|^{2}_{\infty}+\varepsilon\right)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{{B_{r}}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)|^{2s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}\\
&\quad+\lambda^{-n}C^{*}(\varepsilon)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2{B_{r}}}\left(\dfrac{\chi_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}|u|^{p}}{R^{p}}\omega^{p}\right)^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}\\
&\quad+\lambda^{-n}C^{*}(\varepsilon)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2{B_{r}}}|\chi_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}\mathcal{V}(x,F)|^{2s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}.\end{split}

For
I_{2}
, we have

(3.91)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{2}&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\lambda\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla v)-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla v)}\rangle_{\lambda\Omega_{r}}|^{2}\,dx+\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\lambda\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla v)-\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla v)|^{2}\,dx\\
&\lesssim\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\lambda\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla v)-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla v)}\rangle_{\lambda\Omega_{r}}|^{2}\,dx+\lambda^{-n}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla v)-\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla v)|^{2}\,dx\\
&=:I_{2,1}+I_{2,2}.\end{split}

With the help of (
3.72
) in Proposition
3.9
, it follows that

(3.92)

\displaystyle I_{2,1}\lesssim\lambda^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla v)-\langle{\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla v)}\rangle_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}|^{2}\,dx+\|\nabla\psi\|^{2}_{\infty}\lambda^{-n}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}|\nabla v|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\,dx.

Triangle inequalities yield

(3.93)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{2,1}&\lesssim\lambda^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)-\langle{\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)}\rangle_{{B_{r}}}|^{2}\,dx+\lambda^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla z)-\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla v)|^{2}\,dx\\
&\quad+\lambda^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)-\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla z)|^{2}\,dx+\lambda^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}\Omega_{r}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla v)-\mathcal{V}_{{B_{r}}}(\nabla v)|^{2}\,dx\\
&\quad+\,\|\nabla\psi\|^{2}_{\infty}\lambda^{-n}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{4}\Omega_{r}}|\nabla v|^{p}\omega^{p}_{{B_{r}}}\,dx\\
&=:I_{2,1,0}+I_{2,1,1}+I_{2,1,2}+I_{2,1,3}+I_{2,1,4}.\end{split}

To estimate
I_{2,1,0}
, by
z=|\nabla z|=0
in
{B_{r}}\setminus\Omega
and
|\Omega_{r}|\eqsim|{B_{r}}|
due to (
2.4
), we have

(3.94)

\displaystyle I_{2,1,0}\lesssim\lambda^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{{B_{r}}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)-\langle{\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)}\rangle_{{B_{r}}}|^{2}\,dx.

Besides, using the similar argument for
I_{3,1},I_{3,2}
and
I_{3,3}
, we estimate
I_{2,1,1},I_{2,1,2}
and
I_{2,1,3}
, respectively, with replacing the factor
\lambda^{-n}
with
\lambda^{2\alpha}
. For
I_{2,1,4}
, by the same argument as in (
3.85
)–(
3.87
), we have

(3.95)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{2,1,4}&\lesssim\|\nabla\psi\|^{2}_{\infty}\lambda^{-n}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{{B_{r}}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)|^{2s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}.\end{split}

On the other hand, for
I_{2,2}
we apply the same estimate for
I_{3,3}
. Finally, we have

(3.96)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{1}&\leq I_{2}+I_{3}\\
&\leq c\lambda^{2\alpha}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{{B_{r}}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)-\langle{\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)}\rangle_{{B_{r}}}|^{2}\,dx\\
&\quad+c\lambda^{-n}\left(\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|^{2}_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(4{B_{r}})}+\|\nabla\psi\|^{2}_{\infty}+\varepsilon\right)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{{B_{r}}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)|^{2s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}\\
&\quad+c\lambda^{-n}C^{*}(\varepsilon)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2{B_{r}}}\left(\dfrac{\chi_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}|u|^{p}}{R^{p}}\omega^{p}\right)^{s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}\\
&\quad+c\lambda^{-n}C^{*}(\varepsilon)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2{B_{r}}}|\chi_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}\mathcal{V}(x,F)|^{2s}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{s}}\end{split}

for some
c=c(n,p,\Lambda)
. We select a small
\lambda=\lambda(n,p,\Lambda)\in(0,\frac{1}{80})
such that
c\lambda^{2\alpha}\leq\frac{1}{4}
holds, so that we get (
3.79
).
∎

We define the Hardy-Littlewood maximal function and the sharp maximal function for
f\in L^{1}_{{\mathrm{loc}}}
and
\varrho\in[1,\infty)
by

(3.97)

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{\varrho}f(x):=\sup_{r>0}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{r}(x)}|f|^{\varrho}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{\varrho}},\quad\mathcal{M}^{\sharp}_{\varrho}f(x):=\sup_{r>0}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{r}(x)}|f-\langle{f}\rangle_{B_{r}(x)}|^{\varrho}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{\varrho}}.

Now, we employ Proposition
3.10
to show the pointwise sharp maximal function estimate, which is more adaptable form to our gradient estimates. Recall that for
B_{0}=B_{R}(x_{0})
with
x_{0}\in\partial\Omega
.

Proposition 3.11
.

Let
z
,
u
and
F
be as in (
3.5
) and (
3.9
). There exists
s=s(n,p,\Lambda)>1
and
\kappa_{5}=\kappa_{5}(n,p,\Lambda)
such that the following holds: If
\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(4B_{0})}\leq\kappa_{5}
and (
2.4
) hold, then for a.e.
x\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
and any
\varepsilon\in(0,1]
, there holds

(3.98)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{M}^{\sharp}_{2}\left(\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)\right)(x)&\leq c\left(\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(4B_{0})}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}+\varepsilon\right)\mathcal{M}_{2s}\left(\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)\right)(x)\\
&\quad+c\,C^{*}(\varepsilon^{2})R^{-\frac{p}{2}}\left(\mathcal{M}_{s}\left(\chi_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}|u|^{p}\omega^{p}\right)(x)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\quad+c\,C^{*}(\varepsilon^{2})\mathcal{M}_{2s}\left(\chi_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}\mathcal{V}(\cdot,F)\right)(x)\\
&\quad+c\,\dfrac{R^{n}}{(R+|x-x_{0}|)^{n}}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{0}}|\mathcal{V}(y,\nabla z)-\langle{\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)}\rangle_{B_{0}}|^{2}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{split}

for
c=c(n,p,\Lambda)>0
.

Proof.

Let
\kappa_{5}
and
s
be as in Proposition
3.10
. Since
\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
,
\mathcal{V}(\cdot,F)\in L^{2}(4B_{0}\cap\Omega)
and
|u|^{p}\omega^{p}\in L^{s}(4B_{0}\cap\Omega)
by Proposition
2.2
, all terms in (
3.98
) are finite for a.e.
x
. Choose
x\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
and denote

(3.99)

\displaystyle I:=\mathcal{M}^{\sharp}_{2}(\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z))(x)=\sup_{r>0}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{r}(x)}|\mathcal{V}(y,\nabla z)-\langle{\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)}\rangle_{B_{r}(x)}|^{2}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

We divide the case for
r\in(0,\infty)
as follows:

(1)

J_{1}:=\{r>0:B_{r}(x)\cap B_{0}\cap\Omega=\emptyset\}

(2)

J_{2}:=\{r>0:\frac{4}{\lambda}B_{r}(x)\subset 4B_{0}\,\,\text{and}\,\,x\in\overline{\Omega}\}

(3)

J_{3}:=\{r>0:\frac{4}{\lambda}B_{r}(x)\subset 4B_{0}\,\,\text{and}\,\,x\not\in\overline{\Omega}\}

(4)

J_{4}:=\{r>0:B_{r}(x)\cap B_{0}\cap\Omega\neq\emptyset\,\,\text{and}\,\,\frac{4}{\lambda}B_{r}(x)\not\subset 4B_{0}\}
.

For
k=1,2,3,4
let us denote

(3.100)

\displaystyle I_{k}:=\sup_{r\in J_{k}}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{r}(x)}|\mathcal{V}(y,\nabla z)-\langle{\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)}\rangle_{B_{r}(x)}|\,dy.

We immediately find
I_{1}=0
since
z=0
in
\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus(B_{0}\cap\Omega)
. For
I_{2}
, we apply Proposition
3.10
with
{B_{r}}=\lambda^{-1}B_{r}(x)
and
\varepsilon^{2}
instead of
\varepsilon
to have

(3.101)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{2}&\leq\frac{1}{4}I+c\left(\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(4B_{0})}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}+\varepsilon\right)\mathcal{M}_{2s}(\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z))(x)\\
&\quad+c\,C^{*}(\varepsilon^{2})R^{-\frac{p}{2}}\left(\mathcal{M}_{s}\left(\chi_{4B_{0}}|u|^{p}\omega^{p}\right)(x)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+c\,C^{*}(\varepsilon^{2})\mathcal{M}_{2s}(\chi_{4B_{0}}\mathcal{V}(\cdot,F))(x).\end{split}

For
I_{3}
, when
r\in J_{1}
, then
I_{3}\equiv 0
. If
r\in J_{3}\setminus J_{1}
, then for
x=(x_{1},\dots,x_{n})
, denote
\tilde{x}=(x_{1},\dots,x_{n}+r)
and consider
2B_{r}(\tilde{x})(\supset B_{r}(x))
. Then since
\tilde{x}\in\overline{\Omega}
and
\frac{2}{\lambda}B_{r}(\tilde{x})\subset 4B_{0}
, we can apply Proposition
3.10
similarly as above with
B=\lambda^{-1}B_{r}(\tilde{x})
and
\varepsilon^{2}
instead of
\varepsilon
and obtain

(3.102)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{3}&\leq\frac{1}{4}I+c\left(\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(4B_{0})}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}+\varepsilon\right)\mathcal{M}_{2s}(\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z))(x)\\
&\quad+c\,C^{*}(\varepsilon^{2})R^{-\frac{p}{2}}\left(\mathcal{M}_{s}\left(\chi_{4B_{0}}|u|^{p}\omega^{p}\right)(x)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+c\,C^{*}(\varepsilon^{2})\mathcal{M}_{2s}(\chi_{4B_{0}}\mathcal{V}(\cdot,F))(x).\end{split}

For
I_{4}
, since
r\in J_{4}
implies
r\geq cR
, and so together with
\operatorname{supp}z\subset\overline{B_{0}}
, we have

(3.103)

\displaystyle I_{4}\leq c\dfrac{R^{n}}{(R+|x-x_{0}|)^{n}}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{0}}|\mathcal{V}(y,\nabla z)-\langle{\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)}\rangle_{B_{0}}|^{2}\,dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Merging the above estimates, taking the supremum for all
r>0
, and absorbing
\frac{1}{4}I
in the estimates of
I_{2}
and
I_{3}
to the left-hand side, the conclusion holds.
∎

3.4.
Proof of Theorem
1.4

Now, we prove Theorem
1.4
, the non-linear case. To extract the sharp dependency of
q
, we apply the following global Fefferman-Stein inequality.

Lemma 3.12
.

[
BDGN21
, Theorem 20]

Let
q>1
. Then for all
f\in L^{q}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
and
g\in L^{q^{\prime}}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
, we have

(3.104)

\displaystyle\|f\|_{L^{q}(\mathbb{R}^{n})}\leq cq\|\mathcal{M}^{\sharp}_{1}f\|_{L^{q}(\mathbb{R}^{n})}

and

(3.105)

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{M}_{1}g\|_{L^{q^{\prime}}(\mathbb{R}^{n})}\leq cq\|g\|_{L^{q^{\prime}}(\mathbb{R}^{n})}

for some
c=c(n)
.

We also need the following lemma, which is from
[
DKS12
]
.

Lemma 3.13
.

Let
B\subset\mathbb{R}^{n}
be a ball and
g,h:B\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
be such that
g,h\in L^{1}(B)
. Suppose that for some
\theta\in(0,1)
, we have

(3.106)

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\tilde{B}}|g|\,dx\leq c_{0}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2\tilde{B}}|g|^{\theta}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta}}+\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2\tilde{B}}|h|\,dx

for any
2\tilde{B}\subset B
. Then for any
\gamma\in(0,1)
, there holds

(3.107)

\displaystyle\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B}|g|\,dx\leq c_{1}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B}|g|^{\gamma}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}+c_{1}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B}|h|\,dx

for some constant
c_{1}=c_{1}(c_{0},\gamma,\theta)
. Here,
c_{1}
is an increasing function on
c_{0}
.

Now, we prove gradient estimate results for the local boundary case, when there is a priori assumption
u\in W^{1,q}_{\omega}(4B_{0}\cap\Omega)
.

Proposition 3.14

(Local boundary estimate)
.

Assume (
2.4
) and let
u\in W^{1,q}_{\omega}(\Omega)
be a weak solution of (
3.5
) with
F\in L^{q}_{\omega}(\Omega)
for
q\in(p,\infty)
. Then there exists
\delta=\delta(n,p,\Lambda)
such that for any balls
B
with
x_{B}\in\partial\Omega
,
r_{B}\leq 4R
and all
q\in[p,\infty)
with

(3.108)

\displaystyle\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(8B)}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{\delta}{q},

there holds

(3.109)

\displaystyle\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}B\cap\Omega}(|\nabla u|^{p}\omega^{p})^{\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\varrho}}\leq\bar{c}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{4B\cap\Omega}(|\nabla u|\omega)^{p}\,dx+\bar{c}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{4B\cap\Omega}(|F|^{p}\omega^{p})^{\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\varrho}}

for some
\bar{c}=\bar{c}(n,\Lambda,q)
which is continuous on
q
.

Proof.

Define
z=u\eta^{p^{\prime}}
as in the previous subsection with
|\nabla u|\omega\in L^{q}(\Omega)
. Let
B_{0}=B_{R}(x_{0})
with
x_{0}\in\partial\Omega
and

\varrho:=\frac{q}{p}\geq 1.

We first claim the following type of reverse Hölder’s inequality:

(3.110)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}B_{0}\cap\Omega}(|\nabla u|^{p}\omega^{p})^{\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\varrho}}&\leq c(q)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}(|\nabla u|^{p}\omega^{p})^{\theta\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta\varrho}}\\
&\quad+c(q)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}(|F|^{p}\omega^{p})^{\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\varrho}}\end{split}

for some
\theta\in(0,1)
.
If
1\leq\varrho\leq s
where
s
is defined in Corollary
3.3
, then the conclusion directly follows from Corollary
3.3
. Hence we only consider the case
\varrho>s
. To prevent constants blowing up as
\varrho
close to 1, we change
s
to
s^{2}
so that
1<s<s^{2}<\varrho
holds.

Let
\varepsilon\leq\min\{\frac{\kappa_{5}}{p},\frac{1}{n}\}
, where
\kappa_{5}
is as in Proposition
3.11
. Under the assumptions
\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(4B_{0})}\leq\varepsilon
and
\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}\leq\varepsilon
, taking
L^{2\varrho}(B_{0}\cap\Omega)
norm
\|\cdot\|_{2\varrho}
to Proposition
3.11
, we have

(3.111)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I:=\|\mathcal{M}^{\sharp}_{2}(\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z))\|_{2\varrho}&\leq c\left(\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(4B_{0})}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}+\varepsilon\right)\|\mathcal{M}_{2s}(\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z))\|_{2\varrho}\\
&\quad+c\,C^{*}(\varepsilon^{2})R^{-\frac{p}{2}}\|\mathcal{M}_{s}(\chi_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}|u|^{p}\omega^{p})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{2\varrho}\\
&\quad+c\,C^{*}(\varepsilon^{2})\|\mathcal{M}_{2s}(\chi_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}\mathcal{V}(\cdot,F))\|_{2\varrho}\\
&\quad+c\left\|\dfrac{R^{n}}{(R+|\cdot-x_{0}|)^{n}}\right\|_{2\varrho}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{0}}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)-\langle{\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)}\rangle_{B_{0}}|^{2}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&=:I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}+I_{4}.\end{split}

Since
|\nabla u|^{p}\omega^{p}\in L^{\varrho}(B_{0})
, it follows that
\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)\in L^{2q}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
and so
I<\infty
.
First, using Lemma
3.12
together with
\mathcal{M}_{2s}(g)=(\mathcal{M}(|g|^{2s}))^{\frac{1}{2s}}
and
\frac{2\varrho}{2s}\geq s\geq 1
, we obtain

(3.112)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathcal{M}_{2s}(\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z))\|_{2\varrho}&\leq c_{s}\frac{2\varrho}{2\varrho-1}\|\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)\|_{2\varrho}\\
&\leq c_{s}\frac{(2\varrho)^{2}}{2\varrho-1}\|\mathcal{M}^{\sharp}_{1}(\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z))\|_{2\varrho}\leq c_{s}\frac{(2\varrho)^{2}}{2\varrho-1}\|\mathcal{M}^{\sharp}_{2}(\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z))\|_{2\varrho}.\end{split}

Thus for
I_{1}
, one can see that

(3.113)

\displaystyle I_{1}\leq c_{3}q\left(\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(4B_{0})}+\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}+\varepsilon\right)I

for some
c_{3}=c_{3}(n,p,\Lambda)
. Here, we choose

\displaystyle\delta=\min\left\{\dfrac{1}{6c_{3}},\frac{\kappa_{5}}{p},\frac{1}{n}\right\}\quad\text{and}\quad\varepsilon=\dfrac{\delta}{q}

so that

(3.114)

\displaystyle I_{1}\leq\frac{1}{2}I

holds. Then we are able to absorb
I_{1}
to
I
. For the remaining term
I_{2},I_{3}
and
I_{4}
, by (
3.105
) one can see that

(3.115)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{2}&\leq c\,C^{*}(\tfrac{1}{q^{2}})R^{-\frac{p}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{M}_{s}[(\chi_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}|u|^{p}\omega^{p})^{\frac{1}{2}}]\right\|_{2\varrho}\\
&\leq c\,C^{*}(\tfrac{1}{q^{2}})R^{-\frac{p}{2}}\tfrac{\varrho}{\varrho-s}\left\|\mathcal{M}_{1}[(\chi_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}|u|^{p}\omega^{p})^{s}]\right\|_{\frac{\varrho}{s}}^{\frac{1}{2s}}\\
&\leq c\,C^{*}(\tfrac{1}{q^{2}})R^{-\frac{p}{2}}\left\|(\chi_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}|u|^{p}\omega^{p})^{s}\right\|_{\frac{\varrho}{s}}^{\frac{1}{2s}}\\
&\leq c\,C^{*}(\tfrac{1}{q^{2}})\left(\int_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}\left(\dfrac{|u|^{p}}{R^{p}}\omega^{p}\right)^{\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2\varrho}}\end{split}

with
c=c(n,p,\Lambda)
, and similarly,

(3.116)

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq c\,C^{*}(\tfrac{1}{q^{2}})\left(\int_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}|\mathcal{V}(x,F)|^{2\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2\varrho}}.

For
I_{4}
, if we assume (
2.4
), then
|B_{0}\cap\Omega|\eqsim|B_{0}|
holds. Then together with the fact that
z=|\nabla z|=0
in
B_{0}\setminus\Omega
, we have

(3.117)

\displaystyle I_{4}\leq c|B_{0}|^{\frac{1}{2\varrho}}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{0}\cap\Omega}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)|^{2}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

On the other hand, by Lemma
3.12
there holds

(3.118)

\displaystyle I=\|\mathcal{M}^{\sharp}_{2}\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)\|_{2\varrho}\geq c\|\mathcal{M}^{\sharp}_{1}\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)\|_{2\varrho}\geq\dfrac{c}{q}\|\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)\|_{2\varrho}.

Summing up, we have

(3.119)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathcal{V}(\cdot,\nabla z)\|_{2\varrho}&\leq cqC^{*}(\tfrac{1}{q^{2}})\left(\int_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}\left(\dfrac{|u|^{p}}{R^{p}}\omega^{p}\right)^{\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2\varrho}}\\
&\quad+cqC^{*}(\tfrac{1}{q^{2}})\left(\int_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}|\mathcal{V}(x,F)|^{2\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2\varrho}}\\
&\quad+cq|B_{0}|^{\frac{1}{2\varrho}}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{0}\cap\Omega}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)|^{2}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

If we assume (
2.4
), then
|B_{0}\cap\Omega|\eqsim|B_{0}|
and the above estimate implies

(3.120)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{0}\cap\Omega}(|\nabla z|^{p}\omega^{p})^{\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\varrho}}&\leq c(qC^{*}(\tfrac{1}{q^{2}}))^{2}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}\left(\dfrac{|u|^{p}}{R^{p}}\omega^{p}\right)^{\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\varrho}}\\
&\quad+c(qC^{*}(\tfrac{1}{q^{2}}))^{2}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}(|F|^{p}\omega^{p})^{\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\varrho}}\\
&\quad+cq\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{0}\cap\Omega}|\mathcal{V}(x,\nabla z)|^{2}\,dx.\end{split}

Since
z=u\eta^{p^{\prime}}
as in (
3.9
), it follows that

(3.121)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}B_{0}\cap\Omega}(|\nabla u|^{p}\omega^{p})^{\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\varrho}}&\leq c(q)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}\left(\dfrac{|u|^{p}}{R^{p}}\omega^{p}\right)^{\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\varrho}}\\
&\quad+c(q)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}(|F|^{p}\omega^{p})^{\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\varrho}}+c(q)\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{B_{0}\cap\Omega}|\nabla u|^{p}\omega^{p}\,dx,\end{split}

where
c(q)\eqsim(qC^{*}(\frac{1}{q^{2}}))^{2}
which is a continuous and increasing function on
q
. Then since
\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(4B_{0})}\leq\kappa_{5}
and (
2.4
) holds, together with Lemma
2.5
, we can apply Proposition
2.2
. Consequently, we have (
3.110
).

Now, using (
3.110
), we next claim that

(3.122)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}B}(\chi_{\Omega}|\nabla u|^{p}\omega^{p})^{\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\varrho}}&\leq c(q)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{4B}(\chi_{\Omega}|\nabla u|^{p}\omega^{p})^{\theta\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta\varrho}}\\
&\quad+c(q)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{4B}(\chi_{\Omega}|F|^{p}\omega^{p})^{\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\varrho}}\end{split}

for all
B\subset\mathbb{R}^{n}
. Indeed, if
4B\subset\Omega
, then we employ
[
BDGN21
, Proposition 22]
to obtain (
3.122
). If
4B\subset(\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus\Omega)
, then (
3.122
) becomes trivial since
\chi_{\Omega}=0
on
\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus\Omega
. Finally, if
4B\not\subset\Omega
and
4B\not\subset(\mathbb{R}^{n}\setminus\Omega)
, we use the similar argument of
Step 3
in the proof of Proposition
3.8
and so we have (
3.122
).

Now, Lemma
3.13
gives us that

(3.123)

\displaystyle\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{\frac{1}{2}B_{0}\cap\Omega}(|\nabla u|^{p}\omega^{p})^{\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\varrho}}\leq\bar{c}(q)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}|\nabla u|\omega\,dx\right)^{p}+\bar{c}(q)\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}(|F|^{p}\omega^{p})^{\varrho}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\varrho}},

where
\bar{c}(q)
is still a continuous and increasing function on
q
. This proves (
3.109
).
∎

Proof of Theorem
1.4
.

Applying the argument of the proof of
[
BDGN21
, Theorem 2]
, we can eliminate the assumption
u\in W^{1,q}_{\omega}(4B_{0}\cap\Omega)
in the statement of Proposition
3.14
. Note that here we used the fact that
\bar{c}(q)
is continuous and increasing function on
q
. Now, by considering Proposition
3.14
for the boundary case and
[
BDGN21
, Theorem 2]
for the interior case, using the covering argument, together with the assumption

(3.124)

\displaystyle\log\mathbb{M}\,\,\,\text{is}\,\,\,\left(\dfrac{\delta}{q},R\right)\text{--vanishing and }\Omega\,\,\text{is}\,\,\left(\dfrac{\delta}{q},R\right)\text{--Lipschitz},

we get

(3.125)

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}(|\nabla u|^{p}\omega^{p})^{\varrho}\,dx\leq c^{*}\left(\int_{\Omega}|\nabla u|^{p}\omega^{p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{\varrho}{p}}+c^{*}\int_{\Omega}(|F|^{p}\omega^{p})^{\varrho}\,dx,

where
c^{*}=c^{*}(n,p,\Lambda,\Omega,R,q)
. Then the standard energy estimate as in (
1.7
) and Hölder’s inequality imply (
1.20
).
∎

3.5.
Proof of Theorem
1.3

Here we prove Theorem
1.3
using the duality argument.

Proof of Theorem
1.3
.

We only show in the case
1<q<2
, since the case
q\geq 2
follows from Theorem
1.4
with
p=2
. As the previous argument, we first prove the local boundary case, and then employ the result of
[
BDGN21
, Theorem 1]
as the interior case to use the standard covering argument. Recall that

(3.126)

\displaystyle-\operatorname{div}(\mathbb{A}(x)\nabla u)=-\operatorname{div}(\mathbb{A}(x)F)

and that
B_{0}=B_{R}(x_{0})
with
x_{0}\in\partial\Omega
. Define
H\in L^{q^{\prime}}_{\omega}(B_{0})
with the following property:

(3.127)

\displaystyle\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}}(|H|\omega)^{q^{\prime}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}}}\leq 1.

Let
aB_{0}\cap\Omega:=B_{aR}(x_{0})\cap\Omega
for
a>0
and
w\in W^{1,2}_{0,\omega}(2B_{0}\cap\Omega)
be the weak solution of

(3.128)

\displaystyle\begin{split}-\operatorname{div}(\mathbb{A}(x)\nabla w)&=-\operatorname{div}(\mathbb{A}(x)\chi_{2B_{0}}H)\quad\text{in }4B_{0}\cap\Omega,\\
w&=0\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\,\,\text{on }\partial(4B_{0}\cap\Omega).\end{split}

Under the assumption that

(3.129)

\displaystyle\lvert\log\mathbb{M}|_{{\mathrm{BMO}}(4B_{0})}\leq\delta\left(1-\frac{1}{q}\right)\quad\text{and}\quad\|\nabla\psi\|_{\infty}\leq\delta\left(1-\frac{1}{q}\right),

by (
3.123
) with the exponent
q^{\prime}\geq 2
and Hölder’s inequality, it follows that

(3.130)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(|\nabla w|\omega)^{q^{\prime}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}}}\leq c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}(|\nabla w|\omega)^{2}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(|H|\omega)^{q^{\prime}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}}}.\end{split}

Here, testing
w
itself in (
3.128
), we have

(3.131)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{4B_{0}\cap\Omega}(|\nabla w|\omega)^{2}\,dx\leq c\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(|H|\omega)^{2}\,dx\leq c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(|H|\omega)^{q^{\prime}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{2}{q^{\prime}}}\end{split}

and so there holds

(3.132)

\displaystyle\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(|\nabla w|\omega)^{q^{\prime}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}}}\leq c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(|H|\omega)^{q^{\prime}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}}}\leq c.

Let
\eta\in C^{\infty}_{0}(2B_{0})
be a smooth cut-off function with
\chi_{B_{0}}\leq\eta\leq\chi_{2B_{0}}
and
\|\nabla\eta\|_{\infty}\leq c/R
. From (
3.128
), we have

(3.133)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I&:=\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}\mathbb{A}(x)\nabla(\eta^{2}u)\cdot H\,dx\\
&=\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}\mathbb{A}(x)\nabla(\eta^{2}u)\cdot\nabla w\,dx\\
&=\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}\mathbb{A}(x)\nabla u\cdot\nabla(\eta^{2}w)\,dx\\
&\quad+\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}\mathbb{A}(x)u\nabla(\eta^{2})\cdot\nabla w\,dx-\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}\mathbb{A}(x)w\nabla u\cdot\nabla(\eta^{2})\,dx\\
&=:I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}.\end{split}

To estimate
I_{1}
, using the equation for
u
in (
3.5
), there holds

(3.134)

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{1}&=\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}\mathbb{A}(x)F\cdot\nabla(\eta^{2}w)\,dx\\
&\leq c\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}\omega^{2}|F||\nabla(\eta^{2}w)|\,dx\\
&\leq c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(\omega|F|)^{q}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(\omega|\nabla(\eta^{2}w)|)^{q^{\prime}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}}}.\end{split}

With the help of triangle inequality and Proposition
2.2
, we have

(3.135)

\displaystyle|I_{1}|\leq\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(\omega|F|)^{q}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(\omega|\nabla w|)^{q^{\prime}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}}}.

For
I_{2}
, by Hölder’s inequality and Proposition
2.2
, we have

(3.136)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|I_{2}|&\leq c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}\left(\omega\dfrac{|u|}{R}\right)^{q}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(\omega|\nabla w|)^{q^{\prime}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}}}\\
&\leq c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(\omega|\nabla u|)^{\theta p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta p}}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(\omega|\nabla w|)^{q^{\prime}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}}}\end{split}

for some
\theta\in(\frac{1}{q},1)
. Similarly, for
I_{3}
, there holds

(3.137)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|I_{3}|&\leq c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(\omega|\nabla u|)^{\theta_{2}p}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta_{2}p}}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}\left(\omega\dfrac{|w|}{R}\right)^{(\theta_{2}q)^{\prime}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{(\theta_{2}q)^{\prime}}}\\
&\leq c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(\omega|\nabla u|)^{\theta_{2}q}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta_{2}q}}\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(\omega|\nabla w|)^{q^{\prime}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}}}.\end{split}

Now, without loss of generality we assume
\theta=\theta_{2}
. Consequently, with (
3.132
) we have

(3.138)

\displaystyle\begin{split}|I|&\leq c\left[\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(\omega|\nabla u|)^{\theta q}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta q}}+\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(\omega|F|)^{q}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}\right]\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(\omega|\nabla w|)^{q^{\prime}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}}}\\
&\leq c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(\omega|\nabla u|)^{\theta q}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta q}}+c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(\omega|F|)^{q}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}.\end{split}

Since
H
was an arbitrary function with (
3.127
) and
(L^{q^{\prime}}_{\omega})^{*}=L^{q}_{\omega^{-1}}
holds, we obtain

(3.139)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}\left(|\mathbb{A}\nabla(\eta^{2}u)|\omega^{-1}\right)^{q}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}\\
&\quad\quad\leq c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(\omega|\nabla u|)^{\theta q}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\theta q}}+c\left(\mathop{}\mkern-3.0mu\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\textstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\textstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.83331pt{\hss$\scriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to5.1215pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\vbox{\hbox to4.82639pt{\hss$\scriptscriptstyle{-}$\hss}}$\hss}}\mkern-3.0mu\int_{2B_{0}\cap\Omega}(\omega|F|)^{q}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}.\end{split}

Since
